Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The homological operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 464. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 790 with 4 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on homological structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 464. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 790. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any homological module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 48. The projective resolution has length at most 845 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.
